## frames (audit / door offer / beats / finals / epilogue prompts)
--- d90 [the Table] ---
The ring of pale light. The too-many-angled faces. The trillions of eyes. The ring waits. Seven doors — some open, some priced beyond you. The choice is which risk you take. ◈ the Feared — 39 distinct monsters faced blow-by-blow. The footage exists, and it plays unasked. (standing: 100) ◇ the Indispensable — The Indispensable needs a full seat — the galaxy must actually need you, not just notice you. ◈ the Beloved — Viewership 39, fan favor 42 — the galaxy's favorite, by the numbers. (standing: 100) ◇ the Witness — You didn't keep the list (0 exposures on record — the trial needs 3). ◈ the Assimilated — Always open. The table takes anyone — that's what makes it a trap, and what makes it safe. (standing: 50) ◈ the Defiant — Defiance 5 on the record. The 'no' is earned. (standing: 40) ◈ the Relay — Stage-3 neural integration. The matrix doesn't care about your kingdom — it cares how deep you are. (sysbond 0) (standing: 40)
  > path:feared :: Walk the path of the Feared
  > path:beloved :: Walk the path of the Beloved
  > path:assimilated :: Walk the path of the Assimilated
  > path:defiant :: Walk the path of the Defiant
  > path:relay :: Walk the path of the Relay

--- d90 [the Defiant] ---
PRICE ONE — THE TRIBUTE SHIPS. "FOOD," the System says. "FOR THE ROAD WE ARE LOSING." Your pantry holds 12,905 kcal. A full harvest would feed the walk for a season — and empty Haven's shelves. How much food leaves with the walk? paid 0 · dignity 2
  > beat:harvest :: A full harvest. (20,000 kcal)
  > beat:half :: Half. The walk eats light.
  > beat:grain :: Not one grain.

--- d90 [the Defiant] ---
PRICE TWO — THE RELAY'S PERSON. The switchboard's old price, now final: "ONE PAIR OF HANDS. FOR THE RELAY." The table names no one. It doesn't have to. You look at Henry — the one the village trusts most — and the price has a face. ◈ YOUR BUILD — The price has a face. There is another currency the table hasn't priced: you. paid 0 · dignity 4
  > beat:name :: Name Henry.
  > beat:noone :: No one. The walk keeps everyone.
  > beat:buyback :: Name them — and buy them back later.
  > beat:build :: Pay this one with what you are, not who you love. (needs dignity 3+)

--- d90 [the Defiant] ---
PRICE THREE — DE-INTEGRATION. "THE SYSTEM," the System says, very quietly, "WILL TAKE BACK WHAT IT LENT." The overlay. The neural warmth. The abilities that felt like magic because they were. What does it take back? paid -1 · dignity 5
  > beat:overlay :: The overlay. One stage down.
  > beat:all :: All of it. Down to skin.
  > beat:tear :: "You'll have to tear it out."

--- d90 [the Defiant] ---
THE LAST OFFER — the walk is priced, the door is open, and the table makes one last offer: a small thing. Your name, in their records, spelled right, forever. "ONE MORE PRICE," the System says, "FOR ONE MORE THING." Saying no twice is the whole game. paid -1 · dignity 7
  > beat:pay :: Pay it. One small price.
  > beat:notwice :: Say no twice.

--- d90 [the Defiant] ---
The ring holds its light. Trillions of eyes. Now — the last choice is yours, and it's live:
  > final:walk :: Walk away.
  > final:terms :: Name your terms anyway.
